                        (function () {
                            var KEY = 'kalshi-chunk-reload-at';
                            function isChunkError(message) {
                                return /Loading chunk [^ ]+ failed|ChunkLoadError|Loading CSS chunk|dynamically imported module|Importing a module script failed/i.test(message || '');
                            }
                            function recover() {
                                try {
                                    var last = Number(sessionStorage.getItem(KEY) || 0);
                                    if (Date.now() - last < 10000) return;
                                    sessionStorage.setItem(KEY, String(Date.now()));
                                } catch (e) {
                                    // Storage is unavailable (blocked/sandboxed), so we can't
                                    // record that we already reloaded. Skip recovery rather than
                                    // reload unguarded — a persistently failing chunk would
                                    // otherwise trap the user in an infinite reload loop.
                                    return;
                                }
                                window.location.reload();
                            }
                            window.addEventListener('error', function (event) {
                                var target = event && event.target;
                                if (target && (target.tagName === 'SCRIPT' || target.tagName === 'LINK')) {
                                    var url = target.src || target.href || '';
                                    if (url.indexOf('/_next/static/') !== -1) recover();
                                    return;
                                }
                                if (isChunkError(event && event.message)) recover();
                            }, true);
                            window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection', function (event) {
                                var reason = event && event.reason;
                                if (isChunkError(reason && (reason.message || String(reason)))) recover();
                            });
                        })();

                        (function() {
                            try {
                                var isChartsPage = window.location.pathname.startsWith('/charts');
                                var chartsColorScheme = isChartsPage && new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('color_scheme');

                                // Check URL-driven forced modes (keep in sync with src/utils/forcedColorMode.ts,
                                // which is inlined here at build time).
                                var forcedPrefixes = [{"prefix":"/policy-center/","mode":"light"},{"prefix":"/market-integrity/","mode":"light"},{"prefix":"/research","mode":"light"},{"prefix":"/indices","mode":"light"},{"prefix":"/wrapped","mode":"dark"},{"prefix":"/highlight/nfl-2025","mode":"dark"},{"prefix":"/app-feedback","mode":"dark"},{"prefix":"/chalamet","mode":"dark"},{"prefix":"/america250","mode":"light"},{"prefix":"/hedging","mode":"light"},{"prefix":"/company-reports","mode":"dark"},{"prefix":"/citizen-debt-forecast","mode":"light"}];
                                var forcedMode = null;
                                for (var i = 0; i < forcedPrefixes.length; i++) {
                                    if (window.location.pathname.indexOf(forcedPrefixes[i].prefix) === 0) {
                                        forcedMode = forcedPrefixes[i].mode;
                                        break;
                                    }
                                }

                                var theme = isChartsPage ? chartsColorScheme
                                    : forcedMode ? forcedMode
                                    : localStorage.getItem('kalshi-color-palette');
                                var isSystemMode = !theme || theme === 'system' || theme === 'accessible-system';
                                var systemPrefersDark = !isChartsPage && !forcedMode && window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches;
                                var isDark = theme === 'dark' || theme === 'accessible-dark' || (isSystemMode && systemPrefersDark);

                                // Tell the browser the page's color scheme so its native canvas + form
                                // controls don't briefly render in the OS preference (which causes a
                                // "flash of dark" on light pages when the OS is in dark mode).
                                document.documentElement.style.colorScheme = isDark ? 'dark' : 'light';

                                if (isChartsPage) {
                                    // Chart widget background differs from standard theme tokens in globals.css
                                    // to match the widget's custom background colors (dark: #0A0C0F, light: #FFFFFF)
                                    var chartsSurface = isDark ? 'rgba(10, 12, 15, 1)' : 'rgba(250, 250, 250, 1)';
                                    var chartsBg = isDark ? '#0A0C0F' : '#FFFFFF';
                                    document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--surface-x10', chartsSurface);
                                    document.documentElement.style.backgroundColor = chartsBg;
                                    document.documentElement.style.colorScheme = isDark ? 'dark' : 'light';
                                }

                                if (isDark) {
                                    document.documentElement.classList.add('dark');
                                } else {
                                    document.documentElement.classList.remove('dark');
                                }

                                // Reserve the persisted Trader Drawer width before the first paint.
                                // React intentionally renders the auth-dependent drawer closed during
                                // SSR/hydration; without this pre-paint class, opening it after hydration
                                // shifts the entire page horizontally.
                                //
                                // userId/userRole are written via setLocalStorageWithExpiration as
                                // { value, expiry } (expiry in seconds). Honor that expiry here — a raw
                                // getItem is truthy even for an expired session that the auth store will
                                // read as logged out and purge, which would reserve the width and then
                                // snap back (the exact CLS this reservation is meant to avoid).
                                var readWithExpiry = function (key) {
                                    try {
                                        var raw = localStorage.getItem(key);
                                        if (!raw) return null;
                                        var parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
                                        if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== 'object' || typeof parsed.expiry !== 'number') return null;
                                        if (Date.now() / 1000 > parsed.expiry) return null;
                                        return parsed.value;
                                    } catch (e) {
                                        return null;
                                    }
                                };
                                var authedUserId = readWithExpiry('userId');
                                var authedUserRole = readWithExpiry('userRole');
                                document.documentElement.classList.toggle(
                                    'user-logged-in',
                                    !!authedUserId && !!authedUserRole,
                                );
                                document.documentElement.classList.toggle(
                                    'user-applicant',
                                    !!authedUserId && authedUserRole === 'applicant',
                                );
                                var persistedDrawer = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('kalshi-trader-drawer') || 'null');
                                var drawerOpen = !!authedUserId && !!authedUserRole && persistedDrawer &&
                                    persistedDrawer.state && persistedDrawer.state.collapsed === false;
                                document.documentElement.classList.toggle('trader-drawer-open-before-hydration', !!drawerOpen);
                            } catch (e) {
                                // no-op
